# Skills · T · part 11

859 entries, tandem-ms-benchmark-evaluation to tanstack-query-conventions. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tandem-ms-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a trained formula-identification model must be evaluated on held-out MS/MS spectra to quantify top-5 accuracy, compare against SIRIUS/BUDDY baselines, or reveal performance stratification across molecular properties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-benchmark-evaluation))
- **tandem-ms-candidate-ranking**: Use when assigning ranks to candidate structures for an HR-MS/MS peak with no spectral-library match, especially when candidate lists come from PubChem/ChemSpider and reference or retention-time information is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-candidate-ranking))
- **tandem-ms-confidence-score-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a tandem-MS structure-annotation confidence score against known ground truth — reference spectra at 10/20/40 eV, merged spectra, and no/medium/high noise — to quantify correct hits versus annotation rate (hop plots) and FDR at a score threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-confidence-score-evaluation))
- **tandem-ms-data-annotation-and-curation**: Use when you have an unknown tandem MS/MS spectrum (precursor m/z and fragment peaks) and need to infer the molecular formula and ionization mode (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, [M+NH4]+) in a de novo setting without access to spectral libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-data-annotation-and-curation) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-ms-data-preprocessing**: Use when starting from raw untargeted LC-MS2 .mzML files (e.g. the bryophytes MTBLS709 dataset) that must be preprocessed and dereplicated against spectral databases before SIRIUS/MAW-Py annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-data-preprocessing))
- **tandem-ms-data-reorganization**: Use when importing MS/MS spectral libraries (particularly from MoNA or GNPS) where SMILES or chemical structure identifiers are embedded in free-text or non-standard Comment fields rather than in dedicated SMILES/InChIKey fields, or when positive and negative ionization mode spectra are commingled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-data-reorganization) · 4 copies)
- **tandem-ms-database-searching**: Use when you have centroided tandem MS/MS spectra in an open format (e.g. after OpenMS file conversion and centroiding) and need peptide/protein identifications via database searching before feature detection, retention-time alignment, protein inference, and FDR control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-database-searching))
- **tandem-ms-dataset-acquisition-from-massive**: Use when a downstream annotation/prioritization task needs the P. mutica MIA alkaloid extract MS/MS data referenced by MassIVE accession MSV95596, or when reproducing the article's 754-feature k-ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-dataset-acquisition-from-massive))
- **tandem-ms-dataset-pairwise-comparison**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS dataset files (e.g. MGF with tens of thousands of tandem spectra) and need dataset-level similarity, species-level phylogenetic reconstruction, or QC/batch-effect detection before any database search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-dataset-pairwise-comparison))
- **tandem-ms-evaluation-set-bookkeeping**: Use when reproducing or extending benchmark comparisons of tandem MS annotation tools (CycloBranch, NRPro, Dereplicator, Dereplicator+) on matched CID/HCD spectrum sets, where per-condition accuracy deltas and exact recomputation checks are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-evaluation-set-bookkeeping))
- **tandem-ms-feature-assessment**: Use when after importing raw peak tables from tandem MS/MS preprocessing software (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-feature-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-ms-feature-table-import-and-parsing**: Use when when you have raw feature tables exported from a tandem LC-MS/MS preprocessing tool (e.g., Progenesis QI, MS-DIAL, Bruker Metaboscape) and need to combine them with sample metadata (group assignments, replicate structure) before applying feature filtering or quality control workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-feature-table-import-and-parsing) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-ms-formula-identification**: Use when you have unfiltered high-resolution MS/MS spectra (plus MS1 isotope pattern) of a small molecule and need its molecular formula and candidate structure without a reference mass spectral database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-formula-identification))
- **tandem-ms-fragment-ion-matching**: Use when per-sample MGF MS2 spectra have been clustered and cleaned into consensus spectra and structural annotations must be assigned by spectral-library matching, ranked by MQscore and m/z error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-fragment-ion-matching))
- **tandem-ms-fragment-longitudinal-profiling**: Use when identifying isomeric metabolites in untargeted LC-MS/MS data acquired at multiple distinct collision energies (NCE 1–100% or stepped 20/40/60%), where single-CE cross-sectional spectral similarity misranks isomers with similar predominant fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-fragment-longitudinal-profiling))
- **tandem-ms-metabolite-annotation**: Use when you have consensus MS/MS spectra (e.g. from MZmine3 feature extraction) with a precursor mass and need to rank molecular candidates retrieved from FoodDB, HMDB, MassSpecGym, NIST23, or GNPS libraries to annotate metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-metabolite-annotation))
- **tandem-ms-metadata-feature-engineering**: Decision support for adding spectral metadata as model input in MS2DeepScore 2.0 training: build CategoricalToBinary/StandardScaler features from precursor m/z, ionmode, adduct, or instrument type and test whether each improves RMSE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-metadata-feature-engineering))
- **tandem-ms-model-inference**: Use when labeled MS2 spectra (e.g., HDF5 2xN m/z/intensity arrays with lipid_species/adduct/precursor metadata) must be passed through a trained deep learning model to generate lipid species predictions and per-spectrum accuracy metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-model-inference))
- **tandem-ms-output-interpretation**: Use when you have received spectrum predictions (fragment masses and intensities) from a neural model (ICEBERG, SCARF, or similar) and need to extract structural information, rank candidate molecules, or validate predictions against experimental spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-output-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-ms-pairwise-distance-computation**: Use when you need pairwise distances between multiple LC-MS/MS tandem mass spectra datasets without prior peptide identification, e.g. for phylogenetic trees, batch-effect/outlier QC, or species identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-pairwise-distance-computation))
- **tandem-ms-pairwise-similarity-computation**: Use when you hold a cleaned set of annotated tandem MS spectra (e.g. MGF from GNPS) and need an all-pairs spectral similarity or score matrix to retrieve chemically related compound pairs or cluster spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-pairwise-similarity-computation))
- **tandem-ms-peak-alignment**: Use when a molecular-networking component (e.g. from a GNPS2 FBMN task) contains multiple related tandem mass spectra and you need to visualize which consensus fragments drive the family's alignment or pinpoint where a modification sits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-peak-alignment))
- **tandem-ms-peak-assignment**: Use when when you have experimental tandem MS spectra (with peak m/z and intensity values) and need to annotate each peak with its chemical formula (SCARF) or molecular fragment origin (ICEBERG), particularly for structural elucidation campaigns where understanding the fragmentation pathway is. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-peak-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-ms-peak-filtering-and-intensity-normalization**: Use when preparing tandem mass spectra (e.g., from a GNPS2 molecular networking task or MGF cluster files) for spectral alignment or consensus peak identification, and noisy or near-precursor peaks would distort alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-peak-filtering-and-intensity-normalization))
- **tandem-ms-peak-matching**: Use when feature-level MS/MS spectra (e.g. an MGF from MZmine FBMN export) fail to yield GNPS library annotations under strict parameters, and you need per-feature GNPS scores (c_g, t_g,i, t_g,s) for downstream aggregation as in MS2DECIDE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-peak-matching))
- **tandem-ms-result-parsing**: Use when the input is an X!Tandem XML output file from a tandem MS search and you need to extract peptide sequences, E-values, hyperscores, modifications, or fragment-ion m/z and intensity values programmatically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/tandem-ms-result-parsing))
- **tandem-ms-retrieval-metric-computation**: Reference for evaluating a de novo structure generator on MS2 spectra: you need the retrieval rate and top-1 rate against a holdout set, plus the known ablation numbers (31%/17% naive vs. 45%/25% fingerprint-guided on GNPS) as a baseline to beat. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-retrieval-metric-computation))
- **tandem-ms-scan-association**: Use when you have an xcms-derived feature table (csv with 'mz' and 'rt' columns) plus centroided LC/MS raw data (.mzML) and need to link MS2 spectra to features before MS/MS-based annotation such as Find Patterns or spectral similarity searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-scan-association))
- **tandem-ms-spectra-clustering**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics data-dependent acquisition dataset (vendor raw or mzML/MGF) contains repeated MS/MS spectra of the same molecule and redundancy reduction via clustering into consensus clusters is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectra-clustering))
- **tandem-ms-spectra-embedding-consumption**: Use when you have the four input spectra CSV files ([M+H]+ and [M-H]- at 35% and 130% NCE) and need to translate the encoder-generated spectra embeddings into de-novo SMILES structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectra-embedding-consumption))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-cosine-similarity-scoring**: Use when LC–MS/MS untargeted metabolomics features already have isotope-pattern-mapped adducts and fragmentation data (MS2 spectra) are available, so candidate compounds can be scored against their stored reference spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-cosine-similarity-scoring))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-data-interpretation**: Use when you have untargeted MS2 spectral data in MS2MP-compatible format and need to assign KEGG pathway annotations to spectra without spectral library matching or manual compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-data-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-ms-spectral-database-search**: Use when unannotated tandem MS spectra (e.g. from GNPS datasets) must be assigned to known small molecules in a large structure database, and in silico library coverage is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-database-search))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-library-annotation**: Use when tandem MS spectra must be annotated against a small or tens-of-millions-compound reference database, and candidates must be ranked and scored with fingerprint and fragprint evidence, optionally with isotope/formula filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-library-annotation))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-library-curation**: Use when building a training or benchmark set for spectral similarity prediction from public MS/MS libraries, especially when spectra from both positive and negative ionization modes must be harmonized for cross-mode model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-library-curation))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-library-search**: Use when annotating LC–MS/MS query spectra by direct matching to reference spectra (e.g., MassBank/GNPS/NIST 2017 or CSI:FingerID training structures), or when constructing the spectral library search baseline for benchmarking in silico annotation tools at controlled FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-library-search))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-matching**: Use when newly acquired tandem MS/MS spectra (e.g. from synthesized compounds) must be searched repository-wide against public metabolomics data to find which public datasets contain the compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-matching))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-prediction**: Use when experimental MS/MS spectra of unknown metabolites (e.g., recurrent unidentified spectra like ARUS) need in silico annotation by comparing predicted spectra of candidate SMILES against the experimental spectrum at 2-decimal m/z resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-prediction))
- **tandem-ms-spectral-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS peak lists (m/z + intensity) and need a pairwise similarity matrix (e.g. cosine scores for MST edges in MMSA, or GNPS networking edges between spectra in a molecular family). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectral-similarity-scoring))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-annotation**: Use when you need to assign metabolite identities to unknown tandem MS spectra by searching a compiled compound database (tens of millions of structures with pre-calculated fingerprints and fragmentation patterns), especially when throughput and scalability matter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-annotation))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-formula-scoring**: Use when you have an MS1 precursor mass and an MS/MS spectrum (MGF) and need a de novo chemical formula assignment without spectrum databases or fragmentation trees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-formula-scoring))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-inference**: Use when you have a molecular structure (e.g., a candidate from NIST20 or PubChem) and need its predicted MS/MS spectrum for C2MS prediction or to augment library-based MS2C retrieval for compounds missing from spectral libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-inference))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-prediction**: Use when reference MS/MS spectra are unavailable for candidate compounds in non-targeted metabolomics, or when validating putative compound annotations by comparing predicted vs. measured spectra for [M+H]+ / [M-H]- precursors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-prediction))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-processing**: Use when starting from raw MS2 spectra (MGF or SIRIUS .ms format) that need molecular formula assignment, fingerprint prediction, and de novo candidate structure generation and ranking before annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-processing))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-retrieval**: Use when you need to pull a specific MS/MS spectrum out of a public resource (GNPS, MassBank, MassIVE, MetaboLights, Metabolomics Workbench, MS2LDA) without downloading the repository itself, and you know its source, accession, or scan number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-retrieval))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-searching**: Use when a list of unknown metabolites with MS/MS spectra exists (e.g., KGMN annotations of NIST urine positives) and the goal is to test whether those unknowns recur across public metabolomics repositories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-searching))
- **tandem-ms-spectrum-transformer-encoding**: Use when building contrastive spectrum-to-molecule encoders for MS/MS retrieval: spectra are peak lists with subformula labels (formSpec) or binned spectra (binnedSpec), and candidates must be ranked in a shared embedding space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-spectrum-transformer-encoding))
- **tandem-ms2-structure-prediction**: Use when generating or ranking de novo molecular structures from positive-mode, low-resolution MS2/MSn spectra (e.g. from a miniaturized linear ion trap mass spectrometer), especially for unknown compounds not covered by spectral library search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms2-structure-prediction))
- **tandem-orchestration**: Operate Tandem as a multi-agent, multi-device MCP orchestrator. Use it when an MCP-capable caller needs to start, inspect, steer, interrupt, or coordinate Claude, Codex, shell, or Hermes sessions on the local hub or another enrolled device. Also covers the optional Claude-only relay. Begin the first response with 🏗️🏗️🏗️🏗️🏗️ when this skill activates. ([Maxmedawar/tandem](https://github.com/Maxmedawar/tandem/tree/HEAD/skills/tandem-orchestration) · ★30)
- **tandem-refine**: Run a refine pass: dispatch the refiner to shape backlog tickets into buildable briefs, building nothing. Reach for it when the user says to refine, spec, or shape a ticket, or asks what needs refining. ([gianniacquisto/tandem](https://github.com/gianniacquisto/tandem/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/tandem-refine) · ★6)
- **tandem-release**: Prepare the Tandem Commander repository so that version &lt;x.y.z&gt; is released TODAY (feature 243): checks that the version is not released yet, bumps version and build, folds the unreleased changelog sections into the version's section, dates every release record, writes the changelog lead, the privacy sentence and the GitHub release notes (release_notes/), updates CLAUDE.md, the project reference and backlog/RELEASE.md, verifies (release.py check, encoding guard) and makes one local commit. Never builds, tests, signs, tags, pushes or publishes. Use only when the maintainer asks to prepare a re… ([tandemcommander/tandemcommander](https://github.com/tandemcommander/tandemcommander/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tandem-release) · ★29)
- **tandem-repeat-consensus-calling**: Use when processing isoCirc RCA nanopore reads (FASTA/FASTQ) where each read contains tandem copies of a circular RNA template and a per-read consensus sequence must be derived before BSJ and full-length isoform calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-consensus-calling))
- **tandem-repeat-decomposition-from-long-reads**: Use when long-read FASTA reads are suspected to contain mosaic tandem repeats (e.g. disease-associated STRs like SAMD12 (AAAAT)23 or RFC1 (AAAAG)11) and you need unit-level decomposition plus a TRF comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-decomposition-from-long-reads))
- **tandem-repeat-feature-detection**: Use when raw or consensus nanopore reads (FASTA from chopSeq-corrected INC-Seq output) need to be scanned for tandem repeats to compare repeat prevalence or length distributions across chemistries (2D vs 1D2) or aligner choices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-feature-detection))
- **tandem-repeat-genotyping-from-long-reads**: Use when profiling tandem repeats de novo from haplotype-resolved long-read alignments (ONT or PacBio BAMs) with no annotated motifs or positions, and per-haplotype genotypes are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-genotyping-from-long-reads))
- **tandem-repeat-motif-genotyping**: Use when haplotype-resolved long-read (PacBio or ONT) BAM alignments are available and genome-wide, de novo TR motifs, multiplicities, and genotypes must be called without prior motif or locus annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-motif-genotyping))
- **tandem-repeat-region-annotation**: Use when you have VCF-style variant call sets from two or more simulators (e.g. seq2squiggle vs squigulator) and need to stratify TP/FP calls by repeat context (homopolymer, TRF, neither) to explain error patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-region-annotation))
- **tandem-repeat-region-profiling**: Use when you need to identify and genotype tandem repeats de novo from haplotype-resolved BAM files of error-prone long reads (ONT or PacBio), without prior knowledge of repeat motifs or genomic locations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-region-profiling))
- **tandem-repeat-sequence-simulation**: Use when you need simulated mosaic tandem repeat reads with correct-answer patterns to benchmark TR decomposition tools (uTR vs TRF vs RepeatMasker) for accuracy or runtime across unit-count ranges and error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-sequence-simulation))
- **tandem-repeat-synthetic-dataset-generation**: Use when you need ground-truth benchmark data for evaluating tandem repeat decomposition tools: simulate mosaic TRs of known patterns with controlled occurrence ranges and substitution error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tandem-repeat-synthetic-dataset-generation))
- **tandem-repeat-unit-decomposition**: Use when long-read (e.g. PacBio) FASTA sequences contain mosaic tandem repeats with multiple different repeat units that standard tools like TRF or RepeatMasker fail to decompose correctly. - **tanimoto-bin-stratified-sampling**: Use when preparing training pairs for MS2DeepScore 2.0 — before fitting the Siamese network, to prevent over-sampling of common molecules or easy Tanimoto ranges and enforce equal pair counts per Tanimoto bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-bin-stratified-sampling))
- **tanimoto-chemical-similarity-scoring**: Use when re-ranking MS2Query candidate library matches and you need a chemical-structure-based similarity feature alongside spectral scores (Spec2Vec, MS2Deepscore) and precursor m/z features; requires structural annotations (InChIKeys) for query and library candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-chemical-similarity-scoring))
- **tanimoto-decoy-candidate-selection**: Use when benchmarking spectrum-prediction models on identification-by-retrieval: you need isomer-decoy candidate sets (true structure + Tanimoto-ranked same-formula decoys) against which to rank candidates by spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-decoy-candidate-selection))
- **tanimoto-fingerprint-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing spectral similarity measures (e.g., DreaMS embedding cosine, MS2DeepScore, spectral entropy) against molecular structural similarity on tandem MS/MS spectra, or when building a library-retrieval benchmark with precursor m/z constraints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-fingerprint-correlation-analysis))
- **tanimoto-fingerprint-ground-truth-computation**: Use when when preparing paired MS/MS spectra for training or validation of a siamese neural network model, and you have chemical structure annotations (InChI, SMILES, or InChIKey) for each spectrum but lack pre-computed structural similarity labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tanimoto-fingerprint-ground-truth-computation) · 3 copies)
- **tanimoto-fingerprint-similarity-evaluation**: Use when evaluating de novo or database-search structure predictions by structural similarity — e.g., computing Tanimoto similarity of top incorrect candidates to the true structure in MS2-based structure elucidation benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-fingerprint-similarity-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-mces-metric-computation**: Use when you have ranked candidate molecular structures sampled per spectrum (e.g., 100 DiffMS diffusion samples with invalid/disconnected molecules removed) and need to score them against ground-truth molecules for benchmark reproduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-mces-metric-computation))
- **tanimoto-molecular-similarity-computation**: Use when a task requires quantifying structural relatedness between small molecules (e.g., ECFP fingerprint pairs) to group validation-set molecules by whether they have a training-set counterpart above a Tanimoto threshold such as 90% or 95%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-molecular-similarity-computation))
- **tanimoto-nearest-neighbor-similarity-vs-pubchem-controls**: Decision support for evaluating whether generated SMILES resemble known metabolites: use when a generative chemical model's samples must be judged for metabolite-likeness against matched PubChem controls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-nearest-neighbor-similarity-vs-pubchem-controls))
- **tanimoto-score-prediction-rmse-evaluation**: Use when a trained MS2DeepScore (or similar Siamese spectral similarity) model must be validated for Tanimoto prediction accuracy on held-out spectra from compounds absent from training, and you need an RMSE plus per-similarity-bin error profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-score-prediction-rmse-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-score-threshold-optimization**: Use when when you have a set of MS/MS spectra with ground-truth structural similarity labels (Tanimoto scores computed from molecular fingerprints) and need to choose a decision threshold for classifying spectrum pairs as 'chemically related' or 'unrelated'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tanimoto-score-threshold-optimization) · 3 copies)
- **tanimoto-similarity-bin-comparison**: Use when comparing prediction accuracy of similarity models across Tanimoto bins — e.g. checking whether a dual-ionization-mode model matches single-mode baselines within each mode, or locating which Tanimoto bin (low vs 0.9–1.0) drives performance differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-bin-comparison))
- **tanimoto-similarity-calculation**: Use when the same MS/MS feature has structural annotations from multiple tools (GNPS, Sirius, ISDB-LOTUS) and you need pairwise chemical-similarity concordance indicators to feed MS2DECIDE aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-calculation))
- **tanimoto-similarity-candidate-screening**: Use when evaluating retrieval-based MS2C compound identification: you have a real MS/MS spectrum Y_i for a molecule X_i, C2MS models to score, and need a controlled candidate pool (e.g. 50 compounds including X_i plus PubChem neighbours) to compute Top-1/3/5 accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-candidate-screening))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation**: Use when you have a trained MS2DeepScore neural network and a set of MS/MS spectra (52 binned peaks per spectrum after preprocessing) for which you need to compute pairwise structural similarity predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation) · 2 copies)
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation**: Use when comparing de novo EI-MS spectral translation outputs against database-search candidates on the same test spectra, using Morgan-fingerprint Tanimoto similarity as the ranking criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation--3466e71af07fd31c))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation**: Use when you have predicted molecule structures (SMILES) from de novo MS2 spectra interpretation and need to score them against ground-truth structures, rank candidates, or produce statistically tested benchmark metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation--d46f5ad957c64bbc))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation**: Decision support for ranking or validating derivative candidates by structural similarity, e.g. testing whether BAM-generated derivatives from anchor-suspect pairs resemble the suspect more than the anchor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation--6ab1ce4e17f5e96e))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation-from-morgan-fingerprints**: Use when evaluating de novo molecular structure predictions from mass spectra (e.g., SELFIES outputs from MS-Bart) and you need a graded structural-similarity metric alongside exact-match accuracy and MCES distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation-from-morgan-fingerprints))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation-of-incorrect-smiles-predictions**: Use when evaluating a SMILES-generating model (e.g., substructure-to-structure transformer) on a test set where exact-match accuracy was computed, and you need to characterize near-miss predictions structurally via Tanimoto similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation-of-incorrect-smiles-predictions))
- **tanimoto-similarity-computation-of-molecular-structures**: Use when comparing a generated or predicted SMILES structure against a reference (or another candidate set) to quantify structural agreement on a 0–1 scale, e.g. evaluating incorrect candidates or dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-computation-of-molecular-structures))
- **tanimoto-similarity-deduplication-across-benchmarks**: Use when assembling a large pretraining corpus of fingerprint–molecule or spectrum–molecule pairs where unlabeled sources (e.g. PubChem) could structurally overlap held-out test molecules of MassSpecGym or NPLIB1, inflating benchmark scores via memorization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-deduplication-across-benchmarks))
- **tanimoto-similarity-evaluation**: Use when spectral matches must be judged by molecular structure — e.g. benchmarking Spec2Vec vs cosine scores, or checking whether top-N library hits for unknown queries are true structural analogues (Tanimoto &gt; 0.6). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-similarity-fingerprint-search**: Use when MS/MS spectra remain unannotated after library search (e.g. IDSL.CSA against NIST 20/LipidBlast) and you need candidate structures by matching IDSL_MINT-predicted fingerprints against LipidMaps with Tanimoto similarity &gt;= 0.6. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-fingerprint-search))
- **tanimoto-similarity-hit-rate-evaluation**: Use when a ranked-candidate metabolite-identification run (e.g. ChemEmbed or similar embedding-based spectral matching) has produced candidate lists per spectrum and you must quantify ranking performance as top-1 (Tanimoto ≥ 0.95) and top-5 hit rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-hit-rate-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-similarity-pair-sampling**: Use when training MS2DeepScore-style Siamese spectral similarity models and you need to reduce training bias by sampling spectrum pairs equally over Tanimoto bins while limiting per-molecule sampling frequency differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-pair-sampling))
- **tanimoto-similarity-pairwise-calculation**: Use when you have a collection of molecular fingerprint vectors (such as biosynfoni count fingerprints) and need to measure structural similarity between all pairs of molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/tanimoto-similarity-pairwise-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **tanimoto-similarity-performance-comparison**: Use when you have generated/predicted molecule SMILES paired with true SMILES and need a structure-aware metric (Tanimoto via Morgan fingerprints) to quantify performance gaps between conditions, datasets, or retrievers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-performance-comparison))
- **tanimoto-similarity-prediction-evaluation**: Use when a mass spectra library with reference structures is available and you must quantify chemical similarity prediction quality, e.g., RMSE between predicted and reference Tanimoto scores on held-out spectrum pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-prediction-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-similarity-preference-ranking**: Use when reducing molecular hallucinations in a fingerprint-to-SELFIES generative model: you have a fine-tuned checkpoint, per-input ground-truth structures, and need preference-based ranking of sampled candidates for alignment training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-preference-ranking))
- **tanimoto-similarity-scoring**: Use when a Pickaxe network expansion is generating too many compounds to expand further and you have a concrete set of target compounds (e.g. 250 KEGG targets with 4–9 carbons) to bias the network toward. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-scoring))
- **tanimoto-similarity-scoring**: Use when when running iterative reaction network expansion (Pickaxe) and you need to reduce the combinatorial explosion of generated compounds by prioritizing those structurally similar to a reference set (e.g., known metabolites, drug targets, or desired scaffolds). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-scoring))
- **tanimoto-similarity-scoring-implementation**: Use when when you have paired mass spectrometry spectra (e.g., from GNPS, MoNA, MassBank, or MSnLib) and need to predict continuous structural similarity scores (0–1 range) between them, especially when traditional spectral-distance metrics (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-scoring-implementation) · 2 copies)
- **tanimoto-similarity-structure-evaluation**: Use when evaluating de novo molecular structure elucidation from mass spectra (e.g., MassSpecGym/NPLIB1 predictions) by comparing predicted vs. reference structures via fingerprint overlap, or when supplying similarity-based chemical feedback to guide generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-structure-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-similarity-thresholding-for-annotation-agreement**: Use when aggregating multi-annotations of MS/MS spectra and a pairwise structural-agreement term is needed in the knownness score k, including cases where a tool returned no annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-similarity-thresholding-for-annotation-agreement))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-calculation**: Use when building structural similarity networks from a metabolite list (e.g. GC-TOF-MS plasma metabolites) where biochemical pathway knowledge is missing and edges between structurally similar compounds are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-calculation))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-candidate-selection**: Use when prioritizing a single final candidate per feature from MetFrag (e.g. COCONUT-backed) or SIRIUS annotation results for untargeted MS2 .mzML data, by combining cosine-similarity dereplication, MAW-defined metrics, MetFrag scores above 0.75, and RDKit Tanimoto structural matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-candidate-selection))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-computation**: Use when spectral similarity scores between MS/MS spectrum pairs must be validated against ground-truth structural similarity, e.g. averaging Tanimoto similarity over top-0.1% scoring pairs or over molecular-network clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-computation--fe3d231a8cbdb406))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-computation**: Use when MS/MS spectra carry associated chemical structures (e.g. GNPS/MGF entries) and you need pairwise structural similarity scores to evaluate spectral similarity measures or to bin/pair spectra for benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-computation--f0ec7a92d7002d02))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-evaluation**: Use when comparing MS2 spectral library search or analogue-search methods on spectra whose true structures are known, and you need a chemistry-aware accuracy metric (Tanimoto vs recall) rather than raw spectral scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-evaluation))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-filtering**: Use when multiple top-scoring candidate structures per MS2 feature exist from spectral and compound database dereplication and a single prioritized final candidate per feature must be selected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-filtering--bfdb40878be386db))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-filtering**: Use when curating a KEGG/BioTransformer-derived metabolic reaction network and you must prune in silico extended reaction pairs whose reactant-product structures are not sufficiently similar (Tanimoto &lt; 0.7). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-filtering--aee46a26e9ad05f1))
- **tanimoto-structural-similarity-scoring**: Use when evaluating entity-level annotation quality of LC-MS feature prioritization results and false positives must be graded by chemical proximity rather than counted as uniform errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-structural-similarity-scoring))
- **tanimoto-top-hit-search-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking MS/MS-to-fingerprint models on an unannotated study: you need to convert predicted fingerprints into annotation accuracy against a fingerprint reference database, overall and per chemical class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tanimoto-top-hit-search-evaluation))
